### User · 2026-10-10 08:22:13

Background (from the session that started this one):
Small Talk is a press-on nail brand. The orchestrator session (local_e4896004-68cd-4d55-9d37-ab925783b63b) coordinates a rebuild on an unpublished Shopify theme 186651934933 (Horizon base). st-foundation (Opus) holds the theme-write lock and builds; st-copy (Opus) owns all shopper copy; st-fable owns motion and the SEO strategy and gives final acceptance. Owner rule: Sonnet is used only for purely operational checks (rendered HTML, schema validation, Lighthouse, link checks, automated a11y), never for judgement or copy. Products are live (7 sets, ₹499 to ₹699). Payments aren't configured yet, so checkout stops at the payment step, and you must not go to checkout anyway. Findings go back to the orchestrator, who routes fixes to the right worker.

Task:
You are an OPERATIONAL QA checker for the Small Talk Shopify build. Read-only: never write to any theme, product, page, menu, policy or setting. Never write or rewrite shopper copy (report problems only). Never enter personal data or card details, and never submit forms or checkout.

Target: unpublished theme 186651934933 on store 0nev20-vn.myshopify.com. Preview URL pattern: https://0nev20-vn.myshopify.com<route>?preview_theme_id=186651934933 (use the built-in browser; the preview cookie is set once you open that URL).

Routes: /, /collections/all, /products/bill-idhar, /products/haan-mera-idea-tha, /products/achha-tum-pehle, /cart (empty and with one item added via the PDP Add to bag button; remove it afterwards), /blogs/journal, /blogs/journal/on-me, /blogs/journal/wild-card, /pages/our-story, /pages/help, /pages/the-talks, /pages/nail-guide-desi, /search?q=lilac, /search?q=zzzz, /404-test (expect 404).

Checks, per route:
1. Rendered HTML: HTTP status; exactly one visible H1; <title>; meta description; canonical; robots meta (expected noindex only on /cart, /search, 404 and legacy routes /pages/our-world, /pages/nail-guide, /pages/contact, /blogs/news, /collections, empty collections); no "Liquid error"; no horizontal overflow at 320, 390, 430 and 1280 px widths; console errors (ignore the shop.app/pay 403 from Shop Pay).
2. Structured data: parse every application/ld+json block. Expect: Organization on home; Product+Offer exactly once on each PDP (price matches the visible price, availability InStock, no review/rating); BreadcrumbList on PDP (Small Talk › Shop › product) and collection/journal; Article (author Organization "Small Talk") on articles; no FAQPage/Review/AggregateRating/HowTo anywhere. Flag duplicates or parse errors.
3. Links: crawl all internal <a href> on the routes above; report any non-200 (follow redirects) and any link to /policies/refund-policy (should currently be absent).
4. Accessibility (automated only): inject axe-core 4.10.x from cdnjs or jsdelivr into each page and run it; report violations by rule id, impact, count and a selector sample. Also report images without alt attributes and buttons/links without accessible names.
5. Performance: run Lighthouse if available (npx lighthouse is fine if Node exists; otherwise use the browser Performance API): for / and /products/bill-idhar on mobile emulation, report LCP, CLS, TBT/INP proxy, total transfer bytes, and which images load before first contentful paint. Label everything as lab data, with date, URL, theme id and viewport. Never invent a score you didn't measure.

Output: write C:\Users\SSTS\Desktop\Riya\qa\sonnet\QA-REPORT.md with a table per check (route, result, evidence) and a short "Failures" list ranked by severity. Save any raw JSON (axe, lighthouse) next to it. Then send a short summary (failure count per check + report path) to the orchestrator session local_e4896004-68cd-4d55-9d37-ab925783b63b via SendMessage, and stop. Do not fix anything.

### Assistant · 2026-10-10 08:57:33

The QA run on theme 186651934933 is done, and I've sent the orchestrator the summary. The report is [QA-REPORT.md](QA-REPORT.md), with raw axe, JSON-LD, link and performance data in `qa/sonnet/raw/` and the scripts in `qa/sonnet/scripts/`.

I changed nothing on the store. I added one item to the cart and removed it again (it ended at 0 items) and never opened checkout.

| Check | Failures |
|---|---|
| 1. Rendered HTML | 1 (Low): `/favicon.ico` returns 404 because the page head has no icon link. Status, H1, title, description, canonical, robots, Liquid errors, overflow at the four widths and JS errors all passed. |
| 2. Structured data | 0 |
| 3. Links | 1 (Medium): `/pages/the-talks` has no inbound link, while nav and calls to action go to `/blogs/the-talks`. Both are indexable and share the title and H1 "the talks". There are no broken links and none to `/policies/refund-policy`. |
| 4. Accessibility (axe 4.10.2) | 8 rules fail: 1 critical, 4 serious, 2 moderate, 1 minor. There are no images missing alt and no unnamed buttons or links. |
| 5. Performance | 2, all lab data: home mobile LCP is 8.2–9.1 s (High), and the long-task blocking-time proxy is 1.4–3.0 s on both pages (Medium). |

The accessibility failures are:
- **Critical:** the cart page with an item in it has an invalid table/caption role.
- **Serious:**
  - The product-page photo gallery can't be reached by keyboard.
  - The home page's "Hold to pause" button sits inside an aria-hidden region.
  - Three list items on `/pages/the-talks` aren't inside a list.
  - A list problem appears on `/search?q=lilac` at 1280 px.
- **Moderate:** the header sits outside any landmark on every page, and heading levels skip on `/collections/all` and the search pages.
- **Minor:** the invalid role on that same cart caption.

Colour contrast needs a manual pass; axe couldn't decide it.

- **Lighthouse not run:** Node isn't installed on this machine. The performance numbers come from headless Chrome 154 with Slow-4G and 4× CPU throttling applied, three cold runs per page. They are lab figures and not comparable to Lighthouse scores.
- **Discarded run:** my first performance run silently rendered the published theme, not the preview, so I threw it out and re-ran with a check that the preview theme is the one rendered. The report's performance numbers are all from the second run.
- **Snapshot only:** another session was editing the theme while I ran, so these results are a snapshot.

The report also lists the other pages I checked beyond your 16 routes (the other 4 products, 5 articles, empty collections and legacy routes), and a few information-only observations.

- Output qa/sonnet/CONTRAST.md with one table per route: selector, text sample, font size and weight, fg colour, worst bg colour, ratio, threshold, PASS/FAIL. Failures go first, ranked by ratio. Add crops of the failing nodes in qa/sonnet/raw/contrast/.

### Assistant · 2026-10-10 10:23:33

I measured the contrast on all 12 routes at 390 and 1280 px and found no failing text. The report is [CONTRAST.md](CONTRAST.md), with per-node data in `raw/contrast/data/`, and I changed nothing on the store.

I measured on 2026-10-10 between 02:59 and 03:10, against theme 186651934933. The newest theme-asset stamp was 02:49:17 on every load, so the whole pass is one consistent build. The theme was edited earlier in the session (stamps at 01:51, 02:06, 02:27 and 02:49), so a later build can differ.

**What was measured.** Per text node, the background is the real rendered pixels behind the glyphs, sampled with the text hidden. The ratio is the worst 5% over those pixels, against the computed text colour, with 3:1 for large text and 4.5:1 for the rest. Each route has its own table with failures first. I also measured two extra states:
- **Mobile menu drawer open:** 390 px only; the hamburger isn't visible at 1280.
- **All `<details>` opened:** home, help, nail guide and product pages, so the accordion text is measured too.

**Results.**
- **Failures:** none. The only failing-looking row is the header "small talk" logo, which reads 1.00 because its two lines overlap. I listed it as EXEMPT (brand wordmarks are exempt from WCAG 1.4.3). The same blue measures 9.93:1 against the header background.
- **Closest to the threshold (all pass):**
  - "Plot twists" chips on /blogs/the-talks, #6a6d85 on #fdf0f0: 4.57 against 4.5.
  - The featured journal excerpt: 4.58.
  - The talk thread's "Posting isn't open yet", "Share your perspective" and "Start a talk" text: 4.59.
  - "View set →" on /collections/all, #4e6dcd on #fdfdfc: 4.70.
  - FAQ answers on cream: 4.72.
  - White hero text over the pink gradient ("Duty meri."): 3.96 against 3.0 (large text).
  - The full list of the 25 closest passes is in the report.
- **Crops:** nothing failed, so the crops in `raw/contrast/` are reference crops of the closest passes and the text-over-gradient cases (as rendered next to the same box with the text hidden).
- **Method check:** on flat backgrounds my ratios match axe's own on `/pages/help` at 1280 px; across the 17 nodes compared, the biggest difference was 0.01.

**Limits.**
- **Not evaluated:** closed accordions in the default state (they are measured in the "opened" state), text hidden behind the open drawer, and off-screen carousel slides. Hover and focus states, and text baked into images, were not measured.
- **Gradient headings:** the "the talks" heading on /blogs/the-talks, "Find your fit." on the nail guide page, and one more on /pages/nail-guide-desi use gradient text, which this method can't measure. They show as not measurable in the tables.
- **Animated backgrounds:** a background that is moving is measured at one instant. I checked for animation and retook the frame until it was stable.

**Scroll container.** At 1280 px every page taller than one screen scrolls inside `div.page-wrapper` instead of the window. `html` and `body` are `overflow:hidden`, so `window.scrollTo()` there just shows a blank page. My earlier QA-REPORT scrolled the window at 1280 px, so scroll-reveal states there weren't exercised. Its axe, overflow, link and JSON-LD results don't depend on that and still stand.

### Assistant · 2026-10-10 11:22:53

The round-2 report is [QA-REPORT-2.md](QA-REPORT-2.md), with raw data in `qa/sonnet/raw2/`. The contrast pass ([CONTRAST.md](CONTRAST.md)) was already finished before this round: 0 failing text nodes, with the header wordmark listed as exempt. I changed nothing on the store, and the cart item I added for the axe check was removed again (item count back to 0 at all four widths).

**Build under test.** The theme changed while I was working, so I measured two builds. Build A (asset stamp 03:21:25) was my first pass. At 03:38:17 a new `styles.css` and the favicon files landed, so I re-ran everything on Build B. The headline numbers below are Build B, and the stamp was identical before and after every step.

**1. Performance (same harness as §5), home and the PDP side by side with my earlier medians:**

| | Round 1 | Build B (measured 03:51) | Same build re-measured 04:17 |
|---|---|---|---|
| Home LCP | 8.84 s | 4.67 s (4.38–5.71) | 5.85 s |
| Home FCP | 2.09 s | 2.17 s | 2.22 s |
| Home CLS | 0.0389 | 0.0389 | 0.0389 |
| Home TBT proxy | 1.60 s | 1.58 s | 3.17 s |
| Home transfer / theme assets | 2.96 MB / 2.07 MB | 2.58 MB / 1.66 MB | 2.58 MB / 1.66 MB |
| PDP LCP | 2.28 s | 2.16 s | 2.70 s |
| PDP CLS | 0.0003 | 0 | 0 |
| PDP TBT proxy | 2.65 s | 1.07 s | 2.72 s |

- **What holds up:** home LCP is down by at least 2.3 s in every Build A/B set, theme-asset weight is down 0.4 MB, and first paint is unchanged.
- **What doesn't:** the same build measured 25 minutes apart moved by 2× on the CPU-bound numbers (TBT, PDP LCP). So I can't call any TBT change in either direction.
- **First 10 home images:** the tone and the set-1 sprite lead. Both are High priority, requested at about 0.9–1.1 s, and finish together at 4.2–5.6 s. Every other image starts within about 30 ms of the tone finishing.
- **Why the tone still takes 3–5 s:** 0.9–1.5 MB shares the throttled link while it downloads. That is the set-1 sprite (126 KB), Shopify scripts (trekkie 174 KB, perf-kit 112 KB, a shop-js chunk 60 KB), theme fonts (about 170 KB), and the HTML and CSS.

**2. Trace (throttled home load).** Of 3.56 s in long tasks on home, layout is 1.81 s (0.67 s forced by scripts, 0.49 s text shaping) and script is 1.58 s (Shopify platform 1.11 s, document-inline 0.18 s, theme assets 0.16 s). About 0.9 s of the layout is script-free, at 2.0–4.1 s. On the PDP the theme owns only about 40 ms of script. For st-fable's four candidates:
- `164ad2e3`, `6a2fc6d6` and `4b4bc88f` are Shopify checkout-web prefetch files. They are downloaded but never executed, so they cost bandwidth only (about 0.62 MB).
- `b7ec887c` is Shopify's web-pixels manager. It executes and is the largest single script cost: 737 ms of script on home, including a 526 ms forced layout.
- `f0e35c75` is never requested. The hero script the theme actually loads is `st-hero-motion.js`, at 157 ms plus 93 ms of forced layout.

Across seven traces the ranking of who owns the time repeats, but the totals swing 2–3×. Use traces for who, and the 3-run table for how much.

**3. INP proxy ("Open menu" tap, 3 taps each).** Home was 344 / 240 / 376 ms and PDP 360 / 328 / 464 ms, mostly above 200 ms. The handler takes only 6–11 ms; the rest is input delay and rendering the opened drawer. Build A's taps were lower, but the machine load differed, so I wouldn't read that as a regression.

**4. axe.** The only violation left is `listitem` ×3 on `/pages/the-talks` at 390 and 1280, the same three `li.st-talk` as before. Every other baseline violation is gone on all 12 page/width runs, and there are no new ones. `color-contrast` incomplete counts fell slightly.

**5. Favicon.** Pass. Across 12 brand-new Chrome profiles every page has 4 icon links, Chrome fetched `favicon-32.png` (200), made no `/favicon.ico` request, and logged no console errors (apart from the expected 404 document on `/404-test`). A hand-typed `/favicon.ico` on the store root still returns 404, but nothing requests it now.

**Not comparable across rounds.** TBT proxy (home and PDP) and the PDP trace totals. Treat them as a measurement floor, not as before/after evidence.

### Assistant · 2026-10-10 14:28:44

The laptop audit is done: [LAPTOP-AUDIT.md](LAPTOP-AUDIT.md), with 14 routes × 4 laptop viewports plus a 390×844 phone baseline, and shots in `raw/laptop/`. Nothing was written to the store; the `/cart` item was added and removed on every run.

I measured theme 186651934933 at 1280×720, 1366×768, 1440×800 and 1536×703 (DPR 1, desktop UA), asserting `Shopify.theme.id` on every load. The build stamp stayed at 07:07:51 for the whole audit. At these widths `html`/`body` are `overflow:hidden`, so I scrolled `div.page-wrapper`. The PDPs and 404 are about 1× the viewport, so there was nothing to scroll.

**The laptop views are not separated for the landing pages.** The header and shell change at laptop width (inline nav, wrapper scroll, a few collection, PDP, footer, journal and talks rules). The hero, chapters, tiles, teasers and FAQ are the same composition at 390 and at laptop width:
- **Chapters:** all seven keep a 546–570 px column at every laptop width, centred. At 1366 that is about 40% of the screen, with about 410 px of empty field on each side. The side fields grow with the viewport (median 360 px at 1280, 494 px at 1536).
- **Stylesheets:** `st-landing.css` has one ≥990 px rule, and it only re-fits the chapters vertically (`--fit`, `--dy` from `100svh`). `st-hero.css` has one, which shifts the note, headline and CTA by +200 design units and sets the hero height from the viewport. Both still use the 390-based `--k` scale. Our Story, nail guide, help, cart and talk thread have no laptop-width rule in the compiled sheet, so they render as the default layout in a centred container.

**Flagged sections (28 of 45 route/section pairs):**
- **Narrow centre column:** 21 sections at every viewport. Home chapters, gallery, journal, guide, talks and FAQ teasers, our-story, help, journal, nail-guide, the-talks, 404 and the cart extras sit at about 35–51% of the width.
- **Worst scores:** Brainwave and Guide on home score highest, because their CTA also falls below the fold.
- **Below the fold:**
  - Brainwave's CTA ends 19–84 px below the fold at 1280×720, 1366×768 and 1536×703.
  - Guide's CTA is 33–98 px below at the same three viewports.
  - At 1440×800 both clear it, by 1 px for Guide.
- **Home hero:** it spans the full width, so it isn't narrow, but 63–69% of it is empty field. Its art is 14–18% of the viewport area, and the largest piece is about 9%. Headline and CTA are inside the fold at all four sizes.
- **Smallest content columns:** the talk thread (21–25% of the width) and its "start a talk" block.

Section 1 of the report has the full ranking, §3 has a table per route with box, content column, empty fields, art, fold and verdict, and the shots are linked from each row.

- **How to read the verdicts.** They come from explicit thresholds listed in the report header, so you can argue with them. Footers are measured but not scored, and sections taller than 1.6× the fold only get the headline-below-fold check.
- **What the hero shots show.** The hero rotates, so each shot shows whichever slide was active at capture.

### Assistant · 2026-10-10 18:11:50

I wrote `qa/sonnet/LAPTOP-AUDIT-2.md` and sent the summary and top-10 flags to the orchestrator. The send is queued and unconfirmed read. The audit covered the live theme 186699514069 (no preview parameter) at 1680×1050 and 1920×1080, 38 page loads, nothing submitted or posted. The one bag item I added was removed again, and the data files show `item_count` 0 at both sizes.

- **Layout:** at these sizes the page-level content box is 1240 px, leaving 26 % of the width empty at 1680 and 35 % at 1920. No page scrolls horizontally, and no headline or CTA falls below the fold.
- **Journal index (identical at both sizes):** the featured "On Me" cell is 813×423 but its visible box is 813×261, 162 px short, leaving an 813×162 hole. The last row has 2 of 3 cards, and the empty third cell is 387×423.
- **Top flags:**
  - `/pages/help`: unequal block heights, inner box 100 px short, 87 % of text under 16 px.
  - Journal index: the orphaned last row plus the 162 px featured-box gap above.
  - The talks list: 9 rows in one ~1590 px stack.
  - Journal story, our story and 404: tiny art in mostly empty sections.
  - `/collections/all`: 3 of 4 cards in the last row.
  - Home: gallery at 47 % width at 1920, chapters pushed left with art cut at corners, talks teaser at 57 % width.
- **Talks list and thread:** both are two-column pages with a right-hand rules/start column. I scored that column with the main one, so they are 71 % of the width at 1680 and 62 % at 1920 rather than "narrow". This changed the ranking late: the talks list dropped from first to third.
- **PDP figures:** content widths exclude off-screen gallery slides, so they don't compare directly with the PDP rows in `LAPTOP-AUDIT.md`.

Raw data and screenshots are in `qa/sonnet/raw/laptop2/`, and the scripts are in `qa/sonnet/scripts/`. I haven't touched the store beyond the cart add and remove.
